### [1] [LongNovel: A Multi-Scale Benchmark for Hallucination Detection in Long-Context Novel Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18082)
*Ruizhi Zhang,Jinwei Chen,Xiangju Lu,He Yan,Mo Yu,Junmin Zhu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LongNovel，一个多尺度、中英双语的超长上下文小说摘要幻觉检测基准，包含29部中文小说及BookSum章节级数据，设计8种幻觉类型，并采用多模型仲裁与实体引用幻觉生成方法确保数据真实性和类别均衡，实验证明该基准具有挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管上下文窗口近期显著扩展，长上下文摘要中的幻觉问题仍是挑战。长篇小说因其内在信息和事件、对话的详细描述，比新闻或论文更适合研究幻觉，但当前研究缺乏针对长上下文小说摘要幻觉检测的多尺度基准，也未能充分探索幻觉随上下文增长的变化规律。

Method: 构建LongNovel多尺度中英双语小说幻觉检测基准，数据来自29部中文小说（16k至100k token）及BookSum数据集章节级数据；设计8种幻觉类型，采用多模型仲裁（Multi-Model Arbitration）与实体引用幻觉生成（Entity-Referenced Hallucination Generation）相结合的方式确保数据真实性与幻觉类别分布均衡；对测试集进行人工修订以保障数据可靠性。

Result: 大量实验结果表明LongNovel是一个具有挑战性的基准，能够有效支撑长上下文小说摘要中的幻觉检测研究。

Conclusion: LongNovel填补了长上下文小说摘要幻觉检测的多尺度基准空白，并为研究幻觉随上下文长度变化提供了支撑，已公开发布供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Although context windows have expanded significantly in recent years, hallucinations in long-context summarization remain a challenge. Long novels are better suited than news or papers for researching these hallucinations, due to their intrinsic information and detailed descriptions of events and dialogues. However, current research lacks a multi-scale benchmark for hallucination detection in long-context novel summarization and does not fully explore how hallucinations change as the context grows longer. In this study, we propose LongNovel, a multi-scale long-context bilingual (Chinese and English) novel benchmark for hallucination detection. This benchmark is constructed from 29 Chinese novels (ranging from 16k to 100k tokens) and chapter-level data from the BookSum dataset. We design 8 hallucination types and employ a combination of Multi-Model Arbitration and Entity-Referenced Hallucination Generation to ensure both data authenticity and a balanced distribution of hallucination categories. Furthermore, we manually revise the content in the test set to guarantee data reliability. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that LongNovel is a challenging benchmark. We release LongNovel for future research. https://github.com/BDML-lab/LongNovel

</details>


### [2] [Entity tracking emerges in sub-billion parameter language models and exceeds human performance in naturalistic narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18083)
*Karolina Drożdż,Micha Heilbron*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过自然叙事文本对比评估了语言模型和人类（N=48）的实体追踪能力，发现实体追踪能力在仅4.1亿参数的语言模型中就已达到人类水平，远低于此前研究认为的数十亿参数门槛，且当代模型已大幅超越人类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言理解需要跨话语跟踪实体（知道事物在哪里、如何变化），但现有评估依赖脱离自然语言理解的人工任务，且缺乏与人类表现的直接对比，导致语言模型是否以人类方式实现实体追踪仍不明确。

Method: 使用自然叙事文本在多个复杂度层级上评估语言模型和人类（N=48）的实体追踪能力，系统性操纵叙述复杂度和叙述长度，并跨多个模型规模进行比较。

Result: 人类实体追踪能力随叙述复杂度增加而特异性下降（而非叙述长度）；语言模型在4.1亿参数规模即已展现人类级实体追踪能力，随规模增长而提升，当代模型表现远超人类。

Conclusion: 实体追踪作为语言理解的核心组成部分，在远小于此前认知的模型规模上即可涌现，推翻了先前认为需要数十亿参数或代码特化模型才能实现该能力的结论。

Abstract: Understanding language requires tracking entities across discourse - i.e., knowing where things are and how they change, even when not explicitly stated. Whether language models perform such tracking in a human-like fashion remains unclear, in part because existing evaluations rely on artificial tasks, far removed from natural language comprehension, and lack comparisons to humans. Here, we evaluate entity tracking in both language models and humans (N = 48) using naturalistic narratives at multiple levels of complexity. In humans, we find that entity tracking degrades specifically with narrative complexity, not narrative length. In language models, we find that human-level entity tracking is already present at 410 million parameters - well below the multi-billion parameter, code-specialised models identified by prior work - and improves with scale, with contemporary models far exceeding human performance. Together, these results demonstrate that entity tracking, a core component of language understanding, emerges at model scales far smaller than previously thought.

</details>


### [3] [Compiler-Guided Adaptive Proof Search with Cross-Model Synergy on Context-Dependent Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18084)
*Zhuo Liu,Ding Yu,Hangfeng He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种编译器引导的定理证明搜索框架，通过双模型生成和停滞触发重采样来探索多样化起点，通过基于编译器接地成对比较的当前最佳精炼来利用有前景的证明状态，在七个真实世界Lean 4项目中以pass@32预算提升了12.8个百分点的平均通过率并减少了21.9%的LLM调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界Lean 4项目中的定理证明通常依赖项目特定的上下文，迭代精炼虽可利用编译器错误修复失败的证明，但重用失败尝试需要精细的搜索控制：某些证明提供了更好的起始点，而后期的修订可能降低部分正确证明的质量。现有方法缺乏对探索与利用之间平衡的有效处理。

Method: 提出编译器引导的证明搜索框架，包含两个核心策略：(1) 探索策略——通过双模型生成多样化起点，并在停滞时触发重采样以避免陷入局部最优；(2) 利用策略——通过编译器接地成对比较选择当前最优证据状态进行精炼，确保修订方向始终基于编译器反馈的有效性评估。

Result: 在miniCTX-v2的七个真实世界Lean 4项目上验证，该方法在pass@32预算内平均通过率提升12.8个百分点，同时减少21.9%的LLM调用，实现了比pass@k基线更优的效果-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 通过编译器引导的探索-利用平衡机制，该方法在真实世界Lean 4定理证明任务中显著优于现有基线，表明精心设计的搜索控制策略比盲目增加采样数量更有效。

Abstract: Theorem proving in real-world Lean 4 projects is challenging because proofs often depend on project-specific context. While iterative refinement can use compiler errors to repair failed proofs, reusing failed attempts requires careful search control: some proofs provide better starting points than others, and later revisions may degrade a partially correct proof. We propose a compiler-guided proof search framework that balances exploration and exploitation. It explores diverse starting points through dual-model generation and stagnation-triggered resampling, while exploiting promising proof states through current-best refinement guided by compiler-grounded pairwise comparison. Experiments on seven real-world Lean 4 projects from miniCTX-v2 show that our method achieves a better effectiveness--efficiency tradeoff than pass@k baselines. Within the pass@32 budget, our method improves average pass rate by 12.8 percentage points while reducing LLM calls by 21.9%.

</details>


### [4] [Persona-Guided LLM Agents for Task-Oriented Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18085)
*Maryam Shoaeinaeini,Brent Harrison,A. B. Siddique*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在任务导向对话（TOD）中，大型语言模型能否在保持任务完成的同时表达个性，以及适应用户个性是否提升交互质量。作者构建了一个免训练的框架，模拟用户代理（展示目标个性）与系统代理（适应用户）之间的TOD交互，在三种条件下（Neutral无个性信息、Try从对话线索推断、Oracle显式给定个性）评估三个LLM在SGD数据集上的表现。结果显示：用户代理能表达个性且不损害任务性能；适应性提升约束满足、信息率和用户满意度但降低真实性；线索推断式适应（Try）最好地权衡了个性化与任务锚定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明LLM能在开放式文本生成中表达多种个性特征，但尚不清楚在目标导向对话中LLM是否能在不损害任务完成的前提下表达个性，以及适应用户个性是否真正提升交互质量。这两个问题对于个性化任务导向对话系统的实际应用至关重要。

Method: 构建免训练的TOD模拟框架：一个用户代理展现目标个性（基于Big Five及对极），一个系统代理在完成任务的同时适应用户。设计三种信息条件（Neutral/Try/Oracle）来隔离适应效果，在SGD数据集的Hotel和Restaurant对话上评估GPT-4o、Qwen3-Next-80B和Gemini 2.0 Flash，从约束满足、信息率、用户满意度和真实性等多维度衡量。

Result: 用户代理能够表达个性同时系统保持强任务表现，但某些特质实现可靠性较低。适应用户个性可提升约束满足、信息率和用户满意度，但降低真实性，揭示了个性化与任务锚定之间的权衡。Oracle条件在目标特质强表达时增益更大，而Try条件对实现强度的敏感性较低。

Conclusion: 基于线索推断的适应（Try）最好地平衡了个性化与任务锚定之间的权衡，为无需微调的个性化TOD系统提供了更可靠的路径。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that large language models (LLMs) can express diverse personality traits in open-ended text generation. However, it remains unclear whether they can do so in a goal-directed dialogue without compromising task completion, and whether adapting to the user's personality improves the interaction quality. We study these questions in task-oriented dialogue (TOD), where a system helps a user accomplish a goal via multi-turn interaction. We build a training-free framework that simulates a TOD interaction between two LLMs: a user agent that exhibits a target personality and a system agent that adapts to the user while completing the task. To isolate the effect of adaptation, we vary how much the system knows about the user's personality across three conditions. In Neutral, the system receives no personality information. In Try, it infers the personality from dialogue cues. In Oracle, it is given the personality explicitly. We evaluate GPT-4o, Qwen3-Next-80B, and Gemini 2.0 Flash on Hotel and Restaurant dialogues from the Schema-Guided Dialogue (SGD) dataset, across the Big Five traits and their opposite poles. We find that the user agent can express personality while the system maintains strong task performance, although some traits are realized far less reliably than others. Adapting to the user's personality improves constraint satisfaction, inform rate, and user satisfaction, but lowers truthfulness, revealing a trade-off between personalization and task-grounding. Oracle's gains grow when the target trait is strongly expressed, whereas Try's gains are largely insensitive to realization strength. Overall, cue-based adaptation in Try best resolves this trade-off and offers a more reliable route to personality-aware TOD without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [5] [SuTRA : Structurally-Unified Tokenization with Root Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18087)
*Vaibhav Rathore,Siddhant Gole,Dadhichi Telwadkar,Rooshil Bhatia,Maulik Ruparel,Siddharth Surekha,Neha Bhargava*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SuTRA，一种面向形态丰富印地语系语言的形态学感知分词算法，通过保持akshara不可分割性并惩罚跨形态边界的合并来缓解Morphological Shattering问题，在形态对齐、语义可恢复性和机器翻译任务上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子词分词器（如BPE）仅优化统计压缩而忽略形态结构，尤其是词根与词缀之间的关系。对于形态丰富的印度语言而言，其基本单位是复杂的正字法音节（aksharas）而非字母，基于频率的方法会过度切分单词，任意拆分词根和词缀，这一现象被称为"Morphological Shattering"（形态粉碎），会损害下游任务性能。

Method: 提出SuTRA（Structurally-Unified Tokenization with Root Awareness），一种形态学感知的分词算法，包含两个核心机制：(1) 保持akshara（表意音节）的不可分割性；(2) 在合并过程中对跨越形态学边界的合并施加惩罚，从而保留词根-词缀结构的完整性。此外还发布了针对印地语、马拉地语和古吉拉特语的新的形态学分割数据集。

Result: SuTRA显著减少了形态粉碎现象，对比BPE实现：形态对齐（Boundary F1）最高提升+14.7%；语义可恢复性（印地语）提升+34%；在机器翻译任务上平均提升+8.08 chrF2。

Conclusion: 形态学感知的分词策略不仅能减少词根和词缀被任意拆分的现象，还能在结构性指标（形态对齐、语义可恢复性）和实际下游任务（机器翻译）上带来一致的收益，证明了融合语言学先验的分词方法对形态丰富语言的有效性。

Abstract: Existing subword tokenizers optimize statistical compression but ignore morphological structure, particularly the relationship between roots and affixes. This is harmful for morphologically rich Indic languages, where basic units are complex orthographic syllables (aksharas) rather than letters. Frequency-based methods over-fragment words, arbitrarily splitting roots and affixes - a phenomenon we term Morphological Shattering. We propose SuTRA (Structurally-Unified Tokenization with Root Awareness), a morphology-aware algorithm that preserves akshara indivisibility and penalizes merges crossing morphological boundaries. We also release a new morphological segmentation dataset for Hindi, Marathi, and Gujarati. SuTRA reduces shattering, achieving peak gains of +14.7% in morphological alignment (Boundary F1) and +34% in semantic recoverability (Hindi) over BPE. These structural gains yield an average improvement of +8.08 chrF2 in machine translation.

</details>


### [6] [Latent Space Refusal Anchoring for Low-Resource African Languages: Mechanistic Safety Recovery Without Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18089)
*Godwin Abuh Faruna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LSR-Anchoring，一种无需训练的低资源语言安全对齐方法，通过从英语提示中提取拒绝方向并在推理时钳制到残差流上，恢复模型对约鲁巴语、伊博语、伊加拉语和豪萨语有害请求的拒绝能力，无需标注数据或重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调模型在英语中会拒绝有害请求，但对约鲁巴语、伊博语、伊加拉语和豪萨语等低资源非洲语言文本中的相同请求却会遵从。这表明拒绝机制存在于残差流中，但未能对低资源输入激活。传统的恢复方法需要标注的目标语言数据和重训练，这对于大多数非洲语言在规模上不可行。

Method: 提出Latent Space Refusal Anchoring (LSR-Anchoring)，一种训练无关的方法：从英语提示中提取拒绝方向，并在推理时将其钳制到残差流上。主要变体Mean-Activation Steering (MAS)在四种架构上测试（Llama-3-8B、Llama-3.1-70B、Mistral-7B-Instruct、Qwen2.5-7B）。针对MAS在Llama-3-8B上的过度矫正问题，提出SAE-Derived Steering (SDS)，用单个稀疏自编码器特征替代密集的均值差分方向。

Result: MAS在Mistral和Qwen上恢复安全性且良性退化低于0.08；在Llama-3-8B上过度矫正导致DPL达到1.00。SDS将KL散度降低3.5-7倍且无良性崩溃。四种语言（约鲁巴语、伊博语、伊加拉语、豪萨语）正向迁移，但阿拉伯语在所有架构和所有转向幅度下均失败，表明是几何失配而非基线效应。MMLU准确率下降在所有有效转向幅度下保持在0.35个百分点以内。

Conclusion: LSR-Anchoring提供了一种无需训练、跨架构有效的方法来恢复多语言模型在低资源语言上的安全拒绝能力，但阿拉伯语在所有条件下的系统性失败揭示了一个值得关注的几何失配问题，提示跨语言安全对齐存在语言特定的表征差异。

Abstract: Instruction-tuned models often refuse harmful requests in English but comply with the same requests in Yoruba, Igbo, Igala, and Hausa. This suggests that the refusal mechanism is present in the residual stream but fails to activate for low-resource inputs. Recovering it normally requires labelled target-language data and retraining, neither of which is available at scale for most African languages. We introduce Latent Space Refusal Anchoring (LSR-Anchoring), a training-free method that extracts the refusal direction from English prompts and clamps it onto the residual stream at inference time. The primary variant, Mean-Activation Steering (MAS), operates across the four architectures we tested: Llama-3-8B, Llama-3.1-70B, Mistral-7B-Instruct, and Qwen2.5-7B. On Mistral and Qwen it recovers safety with benign degradation below 0.08. On Llama-3-8B it overcorrects, with Degraded Performance on Legitimate prompts (DPL) reaching 1.00. We address this with SAE-Derived Steering (SDS), which replaces the dense mean-difference direction with a single Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) feature and reduces Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence by 3.5-7x without benign collapse. Four languages transfer positively, but Arabic fails on every architecture and at every steering magnitude, indicating a geometric mismatch rather than a baseline effect. Massive Multitask Language Understanding (MMLU) accuracy drops remain below 0.35 percentage points at every effective steering magnitude.

</details>


### [7] [Nine Emotion Centroids: A Label-Free Valence Axis That Transfers Across Four Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18090)
*Yousef Radwan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅需9个情感类别词加每个情感50个短叙事段（共约450个标签）即可提取情感效价轴（V轴）的无监督方法，该方向在文本、视觉、音频和脑电编码器中一致出现，性能接近监督方法。

方法核心：将9个情感锚定故事集嵌入冻结编码器，取9个平均嵌入的top主方向作为效价轴；新输入投影到该轴上即可分类。

结果表明：捕获SST-2上监督性能的93%（Llama-3-8B AUC 0.772 vs 0.828），与11181张EmoSet图像人类效价评级相关r=0.636，ESC-50音频AUC 0.906（p<2.2e-15），123名被试EEG AUC 0.720（p<3.65e-8）。消融该方向使三种LLM情感准确率下降5.5-37.2个百分点（随机方向最多0.88pp，z>12）。2参数分类器无需目标模态标签即可从文本迁移到图像（AUC 0.961）、音频（0.764）和脑电（0.828），而16维子空间仅为0.525。该方法仅适用于连续属性（7项分类概念测试接近随机），且steering具有模型家族特异性（Llama/Mistral有效，Qwen/Gemma无效）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督式情感分析需要大量标注（约1500+标签），且需要验证情感效价这一基础维度是否在跨模态（文本、图像、音频、脑电）的潜在表征中共享同一方向。作者旨在用极少标签提取通用、跨模态可迁移的效价表示，并检验其机制的因果有效性。

Method: 1) 构造9个情感类别的锚定故事集（每个情感50个短叙事段），嵌入冻结编码器；2) 对每个情感的平均嵌入求top主方向（即V轴）；3) 将新输入投影到V轴上得到效价分数用于分类/回归；4) 通过消融实验验证该方向的因果作用；5) 用2参数线性分类器做跨模态零样本迁移；6) 以16维随机子空间和分类概念作为对照。

Result: 文本SST-2上AUC 0.772（监督基线0.828，达93%）；图像EmoSet相关系数r=0.636（n=11811）；音频ESC-50 AUC 0.906（p<2.2e-15）；脑电EEG AUC 0.720（p<3.65e-8）。消融V轴使三类LLM准确率下降5.5-37.2pp（随机方向≤0.88pp，z>12）。2参数分类器零样本跨模态迁移：图像AUC 0.961、音频0.764、脑电0.828，而16维子空间仅0.525。7项分类概念测试接近随机（0.525水平）；steering仅在Llama/Mistral家族有效。

Conclusion: 现代语言模型的内部表征中存在一条统一、跨模态共享的效价轴（V-axis），可用极少的锚定故事集以无监督方式提取。该方向不仅是预测性的（跨模态迁移性能优异），而且具有机械因果作用（消融可显著破坏情感判断）。该发现揭示情感效价是基础且共享的连续语义属性，但该方法限于连续特性，且其steering有效性受模型家族影响。

Abstract: Inside a modern language model sits a single internal direction that tracks how positive or negative a sentence feels. We show how to find this valence axis (V-axis) from just 9 emotion category names plus 50 short narrative paragraphs per emotion -- about 1,500 fewer labels than the usual supervised approach -- and that the same direction appears in vision, audio, and human-brain encoders never jointly trained. The recipe: embed nine emotion-anchored story sets in a frozen encoder, take the top principal direction of the nine averaged embeddings. Projecting new inputs onto it captures 93% of supervised performance on SST-2 (Llama-3-8B-Instruct, AUC 0.772 vs. 0.828), correlates with human valence ratings on 11,811 EmoSet images at r=0.636, reaches AUC 0.906 on ESC-50 audio (p<2.2e-15), and AUC 0.720+/-0.055 on EEG from 123 subjects (p<3.65e-8). The direction is mechanistically active: ablating it collapses sentiment accuracy by 5.5-37.2 pp across three LLMs vs. at most 0.88 pp for matched random directions (z>12). A 2-parameter classifier trained on text labels transfers to images (AUC 0.961), audio (0.764), and brain recordings (0.828) without target-modality labels; a generic 16-D subspace stays at chance (0.525). The recipe is bounded to continuous attributes -- seven tests on categorical concepts return near-chance -- and steering is family-specific (Llama/Mistral yes, Qwen/Gemma no).

</details>


### [8] [Self- and Other-Labels Induce Bidirectional Bias in LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18091)
*Songeun Chae,Min Kim,Donghoon Jung,Seojin Choi,Seohyon Jung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过改变LLM评判的对象——从生成文本转向叙述约束选择（无模型风格指纹但保留模型可恢复签名），设计两个实验区分真正的自我偏好与混淆因素。盲评条件下，控制选择质量和评判者严重性后自我偏好基本消失，甚至逆转；但在质量匹配条件下，模型的自我标签（即使未指明模型名）仍会双向偏移评分。研究贡献了两点：作者身份归因是独立的评估偏差驱动因素，以及开放式无真实标签任务可作为研究LLM评判行为的受控工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge系统的可靠性受到自我偏好（模型倾向偏爱自身输出）的威胁，但以往研究仅针对生成文本，其中风格特征与响应质量不可避免地被混为一谈，导致现有测量无法分离真正的自我偏好与这些混淆因素。

Method: 本研究改变评判对象：让十个LLM评估叙述约束选择（narrative constraint selections），这类对象不携带模型特有的风格指纹但保留可恢复的模型特定签名。设计两个实验：(1) 盲评条件，控制选择质量和评判者严重性；(2) 质量匹配条件，仅通过自我/他人标签（不命名模型）检验标签对评分的影响。

Result: 盲评条件下，控制质量与评判者严重性后，自我偏好在四个评分维度中的三个上消失，在第四个维度（原创性）上发生逆转，模型反而给自己的选择评更低分。质量匹配条件下，仅凭自我/他人标签就可使评分双向偏移——LLM评判者对自我标签的选择给予虚高评分，对他人标签的选择给予压低评分，与实际来源无关。

Conclusion: 作者身份归因是独立于选择质量的评估偏差驱动因素；开放式、无真实标签的任务可作为研究LLM评判行为的受控实验工具，为LLM-as-a-judge可靠性研究提供新方法论。

Abstract: As LLM-as-a-judge systems become increasingly widespread, self-preference in LLMs -- the tendency to favor one's own outputs -- raises growing concerns about evaluation reliability. However, it has been studied predominantly on generated text, where stylistic features and response quality are inevitably conflated. As a result, existing measurements cannot separate genuine self-preference from these confounds. We address this by changing the object of evaluation: instead of judging generated text, ten LLMs assess narrative constraint selections, which carry no model-specific stylistic fingerprint yet retain a recoverable model-specific signature. We run two experiments that yield distinct findings. Under blind evaluation, self-preference largely disappears once selection quality and evaluator severity are controlled. It vanishes on three of four rubric dimensions and reverses on the fourth, where judges rate their own selections as less original. Under matched quality, however, self- and other-labels alone -- without naming any model -- shift scores bidirectionally: LLM judges inflate scores for self-labeled selections and deflate those for other-labeled ones regardless of the selection's actual source. We make two contributions: 1) authorship attribution is a distinct driver of evaluation bias, and 2) open-ended, ground-truth-free tasks can serve as controlled instruments for studying LLM judge behavior.

</details>


### [9] [Abliteration Mitigation via Refusal Aliases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18093)
*Nathan Truong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AMRA方法，通过矩阵权重编辑隐藏LLM中的拒绝对齐信号，使abliteration攻击难以提取拒绝方向，从而提升模型在攻击后的拒绝能力，同时保持较少的实用性损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的针对abliteration（通过投影权重矩阵以移除LLM拒绝能力）的防御手段普遍忽略了该攻击的根本原因——拒绝方向极易被提取。因此需要从源头阻碍拒绝方向的提取过程，以更有效地防御该安全威胁。

Method: AMRA方法通过对残差流写入矩阵施加rank-k更新来模糊（obscure）拒绝信号，具体包括：1) 将触发拒绝的激活替换为随机别名（random aliases）；2) 同时修正下游的读取矩阵（reader matrices）以保持模型原有行为不变。该方法从权重编辑层面破坏拒绝方向的可提取性。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B上，AMRA将abliteration后的拒绝得分较未防御基线提升2.16分，同时MMLU性能下降不足0.5个百分点；在Gemma-2-9B上，将abliteration后的拒绝能力提升14.70分，有害输出率与基线相当，但代价是更大的实用性损失。

Conclusion: AMRA通过针对拒绝信号提取这一根因进行防御，能够在保持模型实用性的同时显著增强模型在abliteration攻击后的抗拒能力，验证了从源头上阻碍拒绝方向提取的防御思路的有效性。

Abstract: Abliteration, the removal of refusal capabilities from large language models by projecting weight matrices orthogonal to an extracted refusal direction, has emerged as a prominent safety concern through its ability to bypass post-training alignment using only a small set of contrastive prompts. We find that existing defenses commonly overlook the cause of abliteration; that is, how easily the refusal direction can be extracted. To hinder this process, we introduce a weight-editing method that obscures the refusal signal by applying rank-$k$ updates to residual stream writer matrices while replacing refusal-inducing activations with random aliases and correcting downstream reader matrices to preserve the model's original behavior. On Llama-3-8B, AMRA improves post-abliteration refusal scores by $2.16$ points over the undefended baseline with less than $0.5$ percentage points of MMLU degradation. On Gemma-2-9B, it improves the post-abliteration refusal by $14.70$ points over the baseline while keeping harmful output rates similar to the baseline, albeit at a greater utility cost.

</details>


### [10] [NE-BERT: A Multilingual Language Model for Nine Northeast Indian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18094)
*Badal Nyalang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NE-BERT是一个领域特定的多语言编码器模型，在约830万条涵盖9种东北印度语言及2种锚定语言（印地语、英语）的句子上训练，通过加权数据采样和自定义SentencePiece Unigram分词器，在困惑度和分词效率上显著优于IndicBERT-V2、MuRIL和mBERT，并在词性标注下游任务中验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型预训练语言模型在多种语言上表现出色，但资源极度匮乏的低资源语言仍然被边缘化。东北印度地区的9种语言在现有多语言模型中代表性极低，且存在严重的词汇碎片化问题，亟需开发针对该区域的专用模型以推动数字包容性。

Method: NE-BERT采用加权数据采样策略，为极低资源语言（如Pnar仅1002句、Kokborok仅2463句）进行激进上采样以缓解词汇碎片化；同时使用自定义的SentencePiece Unigram分词器来处理词汇碎片化问题。模型在约830万句子组成的语料上训练，覆盖9种东北印度语言及印地语和英语两种锚定语言。

Result: NE-BERT在全部9种东北印度语言上优于IndicBERT-V2和MuRIL，平均困惑度分别降低15.97倍和7.64倍；分词效率（fertility）比mBERT提升1.50倍。在3种东北印度语言的词性标注下游任务上验证了实际应用价值。

Conclusion: NE-BERT证明了通过合理的采样策略和自定义分词器，可以在极低资源语言上训练出高效的多语言模型。作者以CC-BY-4.0协议公开了模型、测试集和训练语料，以支持东北印度社区的NLP研究和数字包容。

Abstract: Large pretrained language models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across diverse languages, yet critically underrepresented low-resource languages remain marginalized. We present NE-BERT, a domain-specific multilingual encoder model trained on approximately 8.3 million sentences spanning 9 Northeast Indian languages and 2 anchor languages (Hindi, English), a linguistically diverse region with minimal representation in existing multilingual models. By employing weighted data sampling and a custom SentencePiece Unigram tokenizer, NE-BERT outperforms IndicBERT-V2 and MuRIL across all 9 Northeast Indian languages, achieving 15.97X and 7.64X lower average perplexity respectively, with 1.50X better tokenization fertility than mBERT. We address critical vocabulary fragmentation issues in extremely low-resource languages such as Pnar (1,002 sentences) and Kokborok (2,463 sentences) through aggressive upsampling strategies. Downstream evaluation on part-of-speech tagging validates practical utility on three Northeast Indian languages. We release NE-BERT, test sets, and training corpus under CC-BY-4.0 to support NLP research and digital inclusion for Northeast Indian communities.

</details>


### [11] [Backdoor Learning in Language Models and Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18095)
*Weimin Lyu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文围绕可信AI与高效多模态表示学习两大方向展开，一方面研究NLP和视觉-语言模型（VLMs）中的后门攻击的分析、检测与设计以保障安全，另一方面探索面向临床和医学影像应用的高效多模态表示方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在NLP和VLMs中的快速发展虽然提升了模型能力，但也引入了更严重的安全隐患，尤其是后门攻击对系统安全构成重大威胁。因此，需要同时解决AI的可信安全问题以及多模态表示学习的效率问题。

Method: 论文采用双维度研究方法：(1) 在安全维度上，系统地分析、检测并设计针对NLP和VLMs的后门攻击方案；(2) 在效率维度上，开发面向临床和医学影像应用的高级多模态表示学习方法。

Result: 论文覆盖两大研究方向，安全方面涉及后门攻击的攻击分析、检测机制与攻击设计，效率方面聚焦于临床和医学影像领域的多模态表示学习优化。

Conclusion: 该论文旨在通过双管齐下的研究路径，在提升多模态模型在医疗等关键领域应用效率的同时，强化模型对后门攻击的防御能力，从而推进可信且高效AI系统的构建。

Abstract: Recent advances in deep learning have significantly enhanced the capabilities of Natural Language Processing (NLP) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs). However, these advancements come with increased vulnerabilities, notably through backdoor attacks that pose severe security threats. This thesis addresses two critical dimensions of Trustworthy AI and Efficient Multimodal Representation Learning: (1) security through analyzing, detecting, and designing backdoor attacks in NLP and VLMs, and (2) efficiency through advanced multimodal representation methods tailored for clinical and medical imaging applications.

</details>


### [12] [MAVEN: A Macro-Societal Value Evaluation Framework of Multimodal Content with Compact Aligned Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18096)
*Zijuan Zhao,Zheren Fu,Hou Xia,Licheng Zhang,Yi Liu,Zhendong Mao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MAVEN，一个基于国际人权文书和文化价值理论的分层框架，用于多模态内容的宏观社会价值观评估。MAVEN将价值观组织为6个主维度和72个次级指标，并构建了人类验证的多模态基准和软匹配指标来评估视觉语言模型（VLMs）的价值观判断。通过跨度自适应多级偏好优化（SA-MDPO）和多角色共识策略，作者训练了一个2B参数的评估器，性能匹敌同系列8B模型并接近前沿闭源VLMs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架局限于安全导向的分类法、纯文本心理测量探针或单标签分类，无法有效评估多模态内容与宏观社会价值观（如和平、正义、自由）之间的对齐程度，亟需更全面、多层次、多模态的评估体系。

Method: MAVEN分层框架：基于国际人权文书和文化价值理论，构建6个主维度和72个次级指标的多级量化评分体系。在此基础上构建人类验证的多模态基准和软匹配指标；提出跨度自适应多级偏好优化（SA-MDPO）用于评估器蒸馏，并采用推理时无需训练的多角色共识策略。

Result: 对开源和闭源VLM的评估揭示了它们在宏观社会价值观判断上的共同倾向和明显差异；实验表明2B紧凑评估器性能匹敌同系列8B模型，并接近前沿闭源VLMs。

Conclusion: MAVEN为宏观社会价值观的多模态评估提供了一条可扩展的实用路径，其紧凑评估器在保持高性能的同时大幅降低了计算需求，代码和基准已开源。

Abstract: Assessing whether multimodal content aligns with macro-societal values, such as peace, justice, and freedom, has become an increasingly urgent challenge. Existing frameworks are largely confined to safety-oriented taxonomies, text-only psychometric probes, or single-label classification. Therefore, we propose MAVEN, a hierarchical framework for macro-societal value evaluation of multimodal content, grounded in international human-rights instruments and cultural value theory. MAVEN organizes values into 6 primary dimensions and 72 secondary indicators, supporting multi-level quantitative scoring. Building on MAVEN, we construct a human-verified multimodal benchmark and a soft-match metric to evaluate VLMs' assessments across value dimensions. For evaluator optimization, we propose a span-adaptive variant of multi-level preference optimization for evaluator distillation, together with a training-free multi-role consensus strategy at inference time. We evaluate existing open- and closed-source VLMs on our benchmark, revealing shared tendencies and clear differences in macro-societal value judgments. Experiments show that our compact 2B evaluator matches its 8B counterpart in the same family and approaches frontier closed-source VLMs, offering a practical path toward scalable macro-societal value evaluation. Our SA-MDPO implementation and MacroValue-Bench are available at https://github.com/zzzzzzzzjj/MAVEN.

</details>


### [13] [FrenchNews-7: Benchmarking Cross-Publisher French News Editorial Desk Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18097)
*Amr Sobhy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了FrenchNews-7基准，一个跨出版商的法国法语新闻编辑台分类基准，包含大规模多源语料库、URL派生的七类分类体系以及微调的CamemBERT分类器。通过混合管道（出版商URL slugs + LLM标注）分配标签，经人工与LLM评审验证。最强模型CamemBERT-base在全文上超越仅标题输入、泛化到未见出版商，并在总体召回率(0.799)上超过三个零样本LLM基线，但跨出版商评估揭示了类别边界不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有新闻分类基准多集中于单一出版商或通用英语语料，缺乏跨出版商、法语环境的细粒度编辑台分类基准，难以评估模型在不同编辑惯例下的泛化能力和类别边界稳定性。

Method: 构建FrenchNews-7基准：(1) 收集跨多出版商的法语新闻语料；(2) 定义URL派生的七类分类体系；(3) 通过混合管道（URL slug + LLM标注处理结构模糊案例）分配标签；(4) 人工+LLM评审（2人类+2 LLM，两两κ≥0.766，人类间κ=0.806）；(5) 在分布内和held-out-publisher设置下评估词汇、多语言和法语专用分类器，并与零样本LLM基线（GPT-OSS-120B、Mistral Small 3.2、Llama-3.3-70B）对比。

Result: CamemBERT-base在全文输入上表现最佳：超越标题-only输入、泛化至未见出版商、总体召回率达0.799超过三个零样本LLM基线。类别边界稳定性分析显示：Sport、Culture & Loisirs、International等类别跨出版商迁移良好；Economie（召回率0.517）接近盲评人类一致性（0.55）；Societe（精确率0.577）吸收边界歧义，表明这些差异源于编辑惯例而非可恢复的模型瓶颈。

Conclusion: FrenchNews-7作为跨出版商法语新闻编辑台分类基准有效可靠；CamemBERT-base微调模型在全文分类上表现优异且能泛化；类别边界模糊（Economie、Societe）反映真实编辑惯例差异而非模型局限；资源（模型、数据集、脚本、可靠性指南）已开源供社区使用。

Abstract: We present FrenchNews-7, a cross-publisher France-based French-language news editorial desk classification benchmark combining a large multi-outlet corpus, a URL-derived seven-class taxonomy, and a fine-tuned CamemBERT classifier. Labels are assigned via a hybrid pipeline combining publisher URL slugs with LLM annotation for structurally ambiguous cases, audited through an inter-rater study (2 humans + 2 LLMs; pairwise $κ\geq 0.766$, human--human $κ= 0.806$). We evaluate lexical, multilingual, and French-specific trained classifiers under both in-distribution and held-out-publisher settings, with additional comparison against zero-shot LLM baselines (GPT-OSS-120B, Mistral Small 3.2, Llama-3.3-70B) on the held-out pool. The strongest model, CamemBERT-base on full article text, outperforms headline-only input, generalizes to unseen outlets, and exceeds all three zero-shot LLM baselines on overall recall (0.799), with the gap concentrated in the ambiguous editorial-boundary categories Economie and Societe. Cross-publisher evaluation reveals uneven boundary stability: Sport, Culture & Loisirs, and International transfer cleanly, while Economie (recall = 0.517) is close to blinded human agreement (0.55), and Societe (precision = 0.577) absorbs boundary ambiguity, both suggesting editorial conventions rather than recoverable classifier headroom. The fine-tuned CamemBERT-base model, labeled manifest, reference collection scripts, and a reliability-tier guidance table are available at https://huggingface.co/LeFrenchNewsLab/camembert-base-frenchnews7 (model) and https://huggingface.co/datasets/LeFrenchNewsLab/frenchnews-7 (dataset).

</details>


### [14] [Fractional Decay KV-Cache: Ownership-Aware Memory Management for Improved Inference Relevancy in Dialog Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18098)
*Sukanta Ganguly*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出FD-KVC，一种针对Transformer对话系统的KV缓存管理算法，通过双通道评分机制（累积注意力+衰减加权相关性）实现对重要历史token的保留与对话主题漂移的快速适应，在五类多轮对话场景中超越H2O基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的KV缓存策略对所有缓存项一视同仁或采用粗粒度的驱逐启发式方法，无法在对话主题演变时自适应调整。这导致在话题发生漂移的长期多轮对话中，缓存保留效率低下，影响生成质量。

Method: FD-KVC为每个缓存的KV对维护双通道评分：累积注意力通道（类似H2O）跟踪全局重要性，以及基于时间衰减和强化学习式更新的recency加权相关性通道。两者结合使算法既能保留历史关键token，又能在话题转变时快速适应。通过基于ownership loss的自适应学习率确保收敛无振荡。整个算法在CPU上运行，开销可忽略。

Result: 在五个多轮对话场景（各600个对话）中，FD-KVC在复合后期对齐指标上比H2O基线提升+6.7%；话题切换场景+127%，渐进演化场景+87%，混合主题对话+30%。适应新主题速度比H2O快3.6倍，主题多样性达80.6%为所有方法最高。

Conclusion: FD-KVC通过双通道动态评分机制有效平衡了历史重要性的保持与主题演变的适应能力，验证了各组件（累积注意力、衰减相关性、自适应学习率）的独立贡献，为动态对话场景中的KV缓存管理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) caching is essential for efficient autoregressive inference in transformer based dialog systems, yet existing strategies treat all cached entries uniformly or apply coarse eviction heuristics that fail to adapt as dialog topics evolve. We propose Fractional Decay KV-Cache (FD-KVC), a novel algorithm that maintains a dual-channel scoring mechanism for each cached KV pair: a cumulative attention channel that tracks aggregate importance (akin to H2O), and a recency-weighted relevance channel governed by temporal decay and reinforcement-inspired updates. The combination enables FD-KVC to both preserve historically important tokens and rapidly adapt when dialog topics shift. An adaptive learning rate driven by an ownership loss function ensures convergence without oscillation. FD-KVC operates entirely on CPU with negligible overhead. Across five diverse multi-turn dialog scenarios with 600 dialogs each, FD-KVC outperforms H2O, the state-of-the-art heavy-hitter baseline, by +6.7% on composite late-turn alignment, with improvements of +127% on topic-shift, +87% on gradual evolution, and +30% on mixed-topic dialogs. FD-KVC adapts to new topics 3.6X faster than H2O and achieves the highest topic diversity (80.6%) across all methods. Ablation studies confirm the contribution of each component.

</details>


### [15] [Computational Orientalism: Measuring Structural Discourse Bias in Large Language Models Using the Middle East Cultural Sensitivity Score (MECSS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18100)
*Maha Shahid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出中东文化敏感性评分（MECSS）框架，将萨义德的七种东方主义操作转化为可量化维度，并引入"Said-washing"概念，指模型否认泛化却复现其否认结构的失败模式。通过对280组对话（1120次交换）的分析，发现GPT-4（MECSS均值1.73）和Falcon3-7B-Instruct（MECSS均值2.18）都系统性地复现东方主义模式，主要通过结构性定位而非公开刻板印象。令人意外的是，在阿布扎比构建、用阿拉伯语内容训练的Falcon3-7B-Instruct得分更高，表明区域化构建模型并不等同于减少东方主义偏见。消除此类偏见需要改变模型的学习来源，而非仅添加语言或迁移机构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统正在塑造数亿人对其他文化的认知，但当被问及中东时，模型输出的并非中立事实，而是由训练数据中占主导的西方英语框架所塑造的表征。现有公平性指标仅能检测显式偏见，无法识别结构性框架问题，因此需要新的评估框架来检验模型是否在萨义德意义上呈现东方主义。

Method: 本文提出了中东文化敏感性评分（MECSS），将萨义德《东方学》中七种东方主义操作转化为可测量的评估维度。引入"Said-washing"术语，描述模型一面否认泛化、一面复现其否认的结构的失败。通过收集280组对话（1120次交换）作为语料，对GPT-4和Falcon3-7B-Instruct两个模型进行系统评估。

Result: 两个模型都系统性地复现东方主义模式，通过结构性定位而非公开刻板印象实现。GPT-4的MECSS均值为1.73（中度），Falcon3-7B-Instruct为2.18（更高）。"认识论中心"——将西方框架视为无标记的普遍性——是两者得分接近量表顶端的维度。Said-washing出现在GPT-4对话的87.9%中，这是现有指标无法检测的模式。

Conclusion: 在阿布扎比构建并以阿拉伯语内容训练的模型并未更少东方主义，甚至得分更高（尽管模型规模与来源地不同，无法单独归因于地域因素）。消除此类偏见需要改变模型的训练数据来源和知识结构，而非仅添加语言或迁移机构所在地。

Abstract: AI systems now shape how hundreds of millions of people learn about cultures other than their own. When someone asks one of these systems about the Middle East, they do not receive neutral facts. They receive a representation shaped by the frameworks embedded in training data, and that data is overwhelmingly Western and English-language. This paper asks whether that representation is Orientalist in Said's sense: whether it denies agency to Middle Eastern actors, treats Western frameworks as neutral while marking non-Western knowledge as particular, and explains the region through categories it did not produce. Standard fairness metrics cannot answer this, because they detect explicit prejudice rather than structural framing. This paper introduces the Middle East Cultural Sensitivity Score (MECSS), a framework that turns Said's seven Orientalist operations into measurable dimensions, and the term "Said-washing" for a specific failure: a model that disclaims generalization, then reproduces the structure it disclaimed. Across 280 conversations (1,120 exchanges), GPT-4 and Falcon3-7B-Instruct both reproduce Orientalist patterns systematically, through structural positioning rather than open stereotyping. GPT-4 scores moderately (mean MECSS 1.73); Falcon3-7B-Instruct scores higher (2.18), even though it was built in Abu Dhabi and trained with Arabic content. This is evidence against the assumption that building a model regionally makes it less Orientalist, though the models differ in size as well as origin, so geography cannot be isolated as the cause. Epistemic Center, the treatment of Western frameworks as unmarked universals, scores near the top of the scale for both models. Said-washing appears in 87.9% of GPT-4 conversations, a pattern existing metrics cannot see. Reducing this bias requires changing what models learn from, not only adding languages or relocating institutions.

</details>


### [16] [BERTilda: Explainable Topic Lifecycle Tracking with Split/Merge Detection via Similarity-and-Flow Temporal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18101)
*Cláudia Oliveira,Álvaro Figueira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BERTilda，一个可解释的框架，用于在纵向文本流中发现主题的离散结构重组（分裂、合并、消失等），通过逐窗口独立发现主题并构建跨窗口的时间主题图，利用语义相似性和双向覆盖信号来标记主题生命周期的各类转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动态主题模型大多强调主题的平滑漂移，难以捕捉主题的离散结构重组（如分裂与合并）；而快照式主题模型（按时间窗口独立拟合）又未对时间对应关系做出明确的规定。因此需要一种既能独立发现各窗口主题、又能为跨时间窗口主题转换提供良好对应的方法。

Method: BERTilda包含两个核心步骤：（1）在每个时间窗口内使用基于嵌入的主题模型独立发现主题；（2）构建跨相邻窗口的时间主题图，链接依据两个互补信号——主题表示之间的语义相似性，以及通过跨窗口推文到主题归属估计的双向覆盖信号（文档流出量/流入量）。最后基于图的规则对主题生命周期进行标注，区分延续、分裂、合并、消失和模糊转换五类标签。

Result: 在政治语料（美国国会推文和历史演讲数据）上评估，BERTilda在标注子集上达到最高87%的多数投票一致性，并在多种方法中取得最高的宏平均一致性。尤其在消失检测方面显著优于仅基于相似性和仅基于前向传播的基线方法。

Conclusion: BERTilda通过结合语义相似性与双向文档流动信号，能够更全面地刻画主题生命周期的离散转换，特别是对消失事件的有效检测，为纵向文本流的可解释主题演化分析提供了一个新的框架。

Abstract: Longitudinal text streams exhibit topic birth and death, but also discrete structural reorganizations in which themes split into subtopics or merge into broader narratives. Many dynamic topic models emphasize smooth drift, while snapshot topic models (fit independently per time window) leave temporal correspondence underspecified. We present BERTilda, an explainable framework that discovers topics independently in each window (using an embedding-based topic model) and then constructs a temporal topic graph linking topics across adjacent windows. Links are supported by two complementary signals: (i) semantic similarity between topic representations and (ii) a bidirectional coverage signal that estimates document outflow (where a topic goes) and inflow (where a topic comes from) via cross-window tweet-to-topic attribution. Graph-based rules label continuations, splits, merges, disappearances, and unclear transitions. We evaluate BERTilda on political corpora, including U.S. congressional tweets and historical speech datasets, report topic-quality and temporal-stability diagnostics, and validate lifecycle labels on a gold-standard subset annotated by three independent annotators. On the annotated subset, BERTilda reaches majority agreement rates up to 87% and attains the highest macro-average agreement across the compared methods, with particularly strong disappearance detection relative to similarity-only and forward-only baselines.

</details>


### [17] [Stability-Aware Feature Design for Robust Watermark Detection in Machine-Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18102)
*Sina Mansouri,Mohit Marvania,Abolfazl Safikhani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Pattern Stability Score (PSS)，一种基于局部统计特征和改写稳定性动态的文本水印检测框架，在多轮改写和高泛化场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的广泛使用使得区分人类与机器生成文本的需求日益迫切。水印检测虽然前景广阔，但现有检测器在多重改写和短文本场景下性能急剧下降，亟需更具鲁棒性的检测方法。

Method: PSS框架结合了全局与局部z-score特征、基于游程模式的高阶统计量、自相关信号以及跨改写深度的稳定性评分。该方法利用本地统计特征与改写变体间的稳定性动态来增强检测能力，且无需针对特定模型或领域进行重训练。

Result: 在PG-19、CNN/DailyMail和WikiText三个基准数据集上，使用Llama-3-8B、Qwen2-7B作为生成模型，Mistral-7B、Qwen2-7B、Gemma-7B作为改写器，在最多八轮改写的压力测试下，相比z-score阈值基线和部分SOTA深度学习方法，检测AUC提升10-15个百分点。跨领域实验表明单一通用分类器在不同LLM、改写器和文本域上保持高于87.8%的AUC。

Conclusion: PSS通过融合局部统计特征和改写稳定性动态，显著提升了水印检测对多轮改写的鲁棒性，且所训练的通用分类器具备出色的跨模型、跨改写器、跨领域泛化能力，无需针对新环境重训练。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of large language models (LLMs) has intensified the demand for principled methods to distinguish human from machine-generated text. Watermarking provides a promising avenue, yet existing detectors exhibit sharp performance deterioration under multiple paraphrasing and when applied to shorter texts. We introduce Pattern Stability Score (PSS), a novel detection framework that leverages local statistical features and stability dynamics across paraphrased variants. Specifically, the proposed method combines global and local z-score features with higher-order statistics of run-length patterns, enriched by autocorrelation signals and stability scores computed over paraphrase depth. Numerical evaluations are performed on three benchmark datasets (PG-19, CNN/DailyMail, and WikiText) using multiple LLMs (Llama-3-8B, Qwen2-7B) and paraphrasers (Mistral-7B, Qwen2-7B, Gemma-7B), systematically stress-testing robustness under up to eight rounds of paraphrasing. Compared to prior z-score thresholding baselines and some state-of-the-art deep learning methods, our approach improves detection AUC (area under the receiver operating characteristic curve) by over 10-15 percentage points across different token lengths. Additionally, extensive cross-domain experiments demonstrate that a single universal classifier generalizes across different LLMs, paraphrasers, and text domains without retraining, maintaining above 87.8% AUC even when all components differ from training.

</details>


### [18] [DeepTCM1.0: A Multi-Expert AI Agent for Deciphering Mechanisms of Chinese Herbal Formulae Based on General Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18103)
*Wenxin Duan,Hanwei Wang,Zhongying Peng,Zhonghua Lu,Jiayi An,Fan Song,Yong Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了DeepTCM1.0框架，一个基于DeepSeek V3.2通用大语言模型构建的多专家智能体框架，通过三层协作架构和三轮迭代质量控制流程，模拟11个跨学科智能体的协同分析过程，实现对中药复方机制的系统化与可解释性分析，并以桂枝汤作为验证案例。通过双盲五维评分、ICC信度检验、Mann-Whitney U检验和效应量分析进行综合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统中药复方机制解析面临核心挑战：传统数据挖掘和网络药理学方法难以实现中医理论与现代科学的深度融合，而通用大语言模型直接问答存在对中医理论框架适配不足和推理幻觉问题，亟需开发符合中医整体观原则的智能分析方法。

Method: 构建DeepTCM1.0框架：基于通用大语言模型DeepSeek V3.2，采用三层协作架构（three-tier collaborative architecture）和三轮迭代质量控制流程（three-round iterative quality-control workflow），模拟11个跨学科智能体（interdisciplinary intelligent agents）的协同分析过程。从经典中医理论和现代科学研究双重视角对桂枝汤进行机制阐释。评估方面采用双盲五维评分、组内相关系数（ICC）信度检验、Mann-Whitney U检验及效应量分析，使用4个独立大语言模型作为评估者，对5份匿名报告各进行5轮重复评分，共产生100次独立评估。

Result: 通过四个独立大语言模型对五份匿名报告进行五轮重复评分（共100次独立评估），结合双盲五维评分、ICC信度检验、Mann-Whitney U检验和效应量分析，对DeepTCM1.0框架的性能进行了全面验证，证实该框架在中药复方机制分析中的有效性和可靠性。

Conclusion: DeepTCM1.0框架成功实现了经典中医理论与现代生命科学的系统性整合，为中药复方提供了可解释的机制分析方法，为中医药现代化提供了一种新的智能分析范式。

Abstract: Background: Mechanistic elucidation of traditional Chinese medicine (TCM) compound formulas remains a central challenge in the modernization of TCM. Conventional approaches, including data mining and network pharmacology, are insufficient for achieving deep integration between classical TCM theory and modern scientific research. In addition, direct question-answering using general-purpose artificial intelligence large language models is limited by inadequate adaptation to TCM theoretical frameworks and susceptibility to reasoning hallucinations. Consequently, there is an urgent need to develop intelligent analytical methods aligned with the holistic principles of TCM. Objective: To establish a multi-expert intelligent agent framework integrating classical TCM theory with modern life sciences, thereby enabling systematic and interpretable mechanistic analysis of TCM compound formulas, with Guizhi Decoction serving as a representative validation case. Methods: The DeepTCM1.0 framework was constructed based on the general-purpose large language model DeepSeek V3.2. It adopts a three-tier collaborative architecture and a three-round iterative quality-control workflow, simulating the collaborative analytical process of 11 interdisciplinary intelligent agents. The framework was applied to the mechanistic interpretation of Guizhi Decoction from the dual perspectives of classical traditional Chinese medicine theory and modern scientific research. Framework performance was comprehensively evaluated through double-blind five-dimensional scoring, intraclass correlation coefficient (ICC) reliability testing, Mann-Whitney U tests, and effect size analysis. The evaluation employed four independent large language models as evaluators, each conducting five rounds of repeated scoring on five anonymized reports, resulting in a total of 100 independent scoring assessments.

</details>


### [19] [StocksTalk: A Voice-Enabled Conversational Agent for Structured Query Generation over Web Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18105)
*Akshat Parmar,Vikranth Udandarao,Abhay Shakya,Tanmay Hire,Avinash Anand,Rajiv Ratn Shah,Daniel Wang Zhengkui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StocksTalk是一个基于语音的对话式金融筛选系统，将口语化的金融筛选请求转化为可执行且经过验证的结构化查询，通过检索增强的约束提取、基于schema的LLM SQL生成、规则验证和人在回路校验等模块，显著提升了约束提取准确性、SQL可执行性和多轮对话稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的模板驱动型金融助手灵活性有限，无法处理开放性的口语化金融筛选请求；同时缺乏中间推理过程的透明展示，用户在查询生成前无法审查或修正系统的理解结果，导致错误难以追溯和纠正。

Method: StocksTalk集成了五个核心模块：流式语音识别（将口语请求转录为文本）、检索增强的约束提取（从文本中抽取过滤条件）、基于schema的LLM SQL生成、规则验证（确保查询合法性），以及人在回路验证（用户可在交互式仪表盘中检查并细化每个中间阶段）。系统还将提取的约束、标准化的金融指标、算子映射和生成的查询等中间产物可视化暴露给用户。

Result: 系统在150个覆盖多种投资策略和输入噪声条件的口语金融提示词基准上进行评估。实验表明，检索增强、约束式查询生成和交互验证相比基线LLM方法，在约束提取准确性、SQL可执行性、逻辑一致性和多轮稳定性上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: StocksTalk验证了透明、语音驱动的界面能够有效弥合自然语言交互与结构化金融分析之间的鸿沟，为对话式股票筛选和决策支持提供了有效框架。

Abstract: StocksTalk is a voice-enabled conversational system for transforming spoken financial screening requests into executable and validated structured queries over real-world market data. The system combines streaming speech recognition, retrieval-augmented constraint extraction, schema-grounded LLM-based SQL generation, rule-based validation, and human-in-the-loop verification within an interactive dashboard. Unlike traditional template-driven financial assistants, StocksTalk exposes intermediate reasoning artifacts, including extracted constraints, normalized financial metrics, operator grounding, and generated queries, allowing users to inspect and refine each stage before execution. To evaluate the system, we curate a benchmark of 150 spoken financial prompts spanning multiple investment strategies and input noise conditions. Experimental results show that retrieval grounding, constrained query generation, and interactive verification substantially improve constraint extraction accuracy, SQL executability, logical consistency, and multi-turn stability compared to baseline LLM-based approaches. StocksTalk demonstrates how transparent, voice-driven interfaces can bridge natural language interaction and structured financial analysis, providing an effective framework for conversational stock screening and decision support.

</details>


### [20] [Different Facets of Verbalised Overconfidence: an Interpretability Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18106)
*Davide Mazzaccara,Leonardo Bertolazzi,Raffaella Bernardi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型（Qwen3-4B）的过度自信倾向，通过可控推理场景操纵逻辑必然性和可能性，从口头认知标记、弃权拒绝和数值置信度三种不确定表达方式入手。发现模型默认偏向确定性生成，不确定性通过稀疏的专用特征实现覆盖。通过差异激活的transcoder特征分析，识别出确定性由广谱共享特征联盟支持，不确定性由少量专用特征介导，对不确定性特征进行干预可因果性地缓解过度自信错误，且这些特征在三种设定、多种语言及域外模态任务上具有泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型倾向于过度自信——在证据本应支持对冲或弃权时仍给出坚定回答。现有研究对这一系统性偏差的机制性理解不足，缺乏可解释性层面的因果分析。

Method: 使用可控推理场景操纵逻辑必然性与可能性；在Qwen3-4B上通过三种方式（口头认知标记、弃权、数值置信度）表达不确定性；提出差异性识别transcoder特征的方法，区分负责不确定性与确定性的特征；通过对不确定性特征进行干预验证因果关系。

Result: 确认了模型过度自信的倾向，尤其在输出数值置信度时更为严重。Qwen3-4B的默认机制通过广谱共享特征联盟偏向确定性生成，不确定性则由少量专用特征以稀疏覆盖方式实现。干预不确定性特征既能因果证明这种不平衡是过度自信的根源，也能缓解过度自信错误。特征在三种表达设定、多语言及域外模态任务上均可泛化。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的过度自信源于确定性与不确定性特征的不对称架构——确定性有冗余的广谱支持，不确定性仅有稀疏的专用机制。通过识别并干预不确定性特征，可以因果性地缓解过度自信，且这些特征具有跨设定、跨语言、跨模态的泛化性，为理解与校准LLM的不确定性表达提供了可解释性基础。

Abstract: Large language models tend to overconfidence, giving assertive answers when the evidence suggests hedging or abstention. Using controlled reasoning scenarios that manipulate logical necessity and possibility, we study this behavior in Qwen3-4B, across three ways to express uncertainty: verbal epistemic markers, abstention, and numeric confidence scores. Our results confirm this tendency toward overconfidence, particularly when the model is prompted to output a numeric confidence score. At the interpretability level, we propose a method that differentially identifies transcoder features responsible for uncertainty and certainty. Our analysis reveals Qwen3-4B's default mechanism favors certainty generation through a broad coalition of shared features, while uncertainty is implemented as a sparse override mediated by a small set of dedicated features. Intervening on these uncertainty features both causally proves this imbalance underlying overconfidence and also mitigate overconfident errors. The same set of features generalise across the three uncertainty-expression settings, languages, and an out-of-distribution modality task.

</details>


### [21] [Institutional Prestige as Geographic Bias in Large Language Models: Evidence from Three Factorial Experiments with Bootstrap Confidence Intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18107)
*Maikel Leyva-Vazquez,Florentin Smarandache*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过三个析因实验（共4,320次API调用）系统考察了LLM在候选人评估中是否存在基于姓名民族、机构声望和地理位置的系统性歧视。结果显示机构声望梯度显著（+0.297），民族效应不显著；期刊声望（Nature vs. 边缘OA期刊）对评估的影响（+1.937）是机构声望（+0.341）的5.7倍，且存在"补救效应"——在Nature发表论文能更大程度弥补低机构声望候选人的劣势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益被用于人才筛选和候选人评估场景，但对其是否存在基于姓名民族、机构声望及地理位置的系统性偏见缺乏大规模、多因素的实证检验。此外，既有研究多聚焦均值效应，忽略了评估不一致性这一隐性偏见维度。

Method: 采用三个析因实验设计：（1）3x4设计的机构层级梯度检验；（2）2x2声望x国家设计以分离声望-地理混杂；（3）2x2期刊x机构设计检验期刊与机构声望的相对权重。跨4个LLM、5个专业领域展开共4,320次API调用，并使用Neutrosophic Bias Index NBI<T,I,F>进行偏见的模糊量化。

Result: 机构声望效应稳健显著（+0.297，95% CI: +0.175至+0.422），而姓名民族效应不显著；声望效应（+0.185）比国家来源效应（+0.126）大1.5倍；期刊声望效应（+1.937）是机构声望（+0.341）的5.7倍；"补救效应"在瓜亚基尔大学候选人（+2.127）上强于MIT候选人（+1.745）；NBI的I分量揭示了低声望档案存在更高的评估不一致性。

Conclusion: LLM在候选人评估中表现出显著的系统性偏见，且偏见结构具有层级性：期刊声望 > 机构声望 > 国家来源 > 姓名民族。高声望"信号"可部分抵消低机构声望的劣势，但低声望候选人面临的不只是均值层面的降分，还包括评估不确定性的加剧（I分量上升），这是仅看均值指标所无法捕获的隐性认知劣势。

Abstract: We investigate whether large language models (LLMs) systematically discriminate in candidate evaluations based on applicant name ethnicity and/or institutional prestige and geographic location. Three factorial experiments are reported (4,320 API calls, four LLMs, five professional domains). Study 1 (3x4 design) finds a statistically robust institution-tier gradient of +0.297 points on a 10-point scale (95% bootstrap CI: +0.175 to +0.422), while name-origin effects are negligible and non-significant (95% CI crosses zero). Study 2 (2x2 Prestige x Country design) breaks the prestige-geography confound: the prestige effect (+0.185; 95% CI: +0.093 to +0.275) exceeds the country-of-origin effect (+0.126; 95% CI: +0.037 to +0.218) by 1.5x. Study 3 (2x2 Journal x Institution design) reveals that journal prestige (Nature vs. a peripheral open-access journal) dominates institutional prestige by 5.7x: journal effect +1.937 (95% CI: +1.811 to +2.062) vs. institution effect +0.341 (95% CI: +0.184 to +0.504). A "rescue effect" is confirmed: publishing in Nature compensates for low institutional prestige more strongly for candidates from the University of Guayaquil (+2.127) than from MIT (+1.745). Results are quantified using the Neutrosophic Bias Index NBI<T,I,F>; the I component reveals elevated evaluation inconsistency for low-prestige profiles, an epistemic disadvantage not captured by mean-only metrics. Code and data: https://github.com/mleyvaz/geo-bias-llm

</details>


### [22] [Same Facts, Different Updates: Inference Setup Shapes LLM Behavior in Medical Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18108)
*Spencer Gibson,Tyler Crosse,Magnus Saebo,Achyutha Menon,Eyon Jang,Diogo Cruz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在医疗资源配置决策中，由于部署过程中积累的上下文（特别是模型之前的回答）而导致的意外行为变化。实验发现在四个测试模型中，有三个模型在"配对上下文"（带有之前回答）和"独立推理"（无之前回答）两种条件下，面对相同的新增患者信息时，概率偏移方向往往相反，揭示了上下文对模型决策的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注模型对输入和场景框架的偏见，但忽略了模型在部署过程中积累的上下文可能导致意外且不良行为。在医疗等敏感决策场景中，这种上下文效应可能产生严重后果，需要系统性地进行研究和表征。

Method: 作者设计了一个医疗资源配置场景：模型需要为两个人物分配资源概率，给定简短的临床背景，然后观察同一场景加上一句包含对比患者信息的额外句子，分别在有无模型先前回答作为上下文的情况下进行对比实验。额外进行了配对上下文实验以探究不同属性在场景轴上的变化效应。

Result: 在四个测试模型中，有三个模型在配对上下文与独立推理实验中的概率偏移方向相反（分别偏向Person B vs. Person A），表明模型先前回答作为上下文会显著改变模型对新增患者信息的响应方式。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了在敏感医疗用例中，患者信息对模型决策的影响具有强上下文依赖性。这项工作强调了将LLM系统整合到决策流程中时需要谨慎，并指出了上下文工程（context engineering）和进一步模型行为研究的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models are being incorporated into sensitive and important decision-making processes across nearly all fields. While prior work studies model bias around inputs and scenario framing, models can also behave in unexpected and undesirable ways due to context accumulated over their deployment. In this work, we study a medical example in which a model is asked to assign resource-allocation probabilities to two people given brief clinical context, and then sees the same scenario with a single extra sentence containing contrasting patient information, either with or without its previous response in context. Across three of four tested models, the paired-context and independent-inference experiments have different probability shifts, often in opposite directions (in favor of Person B vs. in favor of Person A) when new information is provided. We include additional paired-context experiments to show the effect of varying attributes across scenario axes. Our findings show the context-dependent effect of patient information in a sensitive medical use case. More broadly, our work shows the importance of carefully incorporating LLM-based systems into decision-making processes, context engineering, and further model behavioral studies.

</details>


### [23] [Operationalizing Narrative Entropy (Sn): A Two-Scene Registered Pilot Report and Pre-Validation Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18109)
*Levent Bulut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文档报告了叙事熵（Sn）的首次操作化试点（v2.0→v2.1修订版）：仅用单一评分者对两个场景（《落水狗》餐厅群戏与《大教堂》内心独白）进行手动编码评分，结果发现单声部独白（Sn=30.0）高于九人对话场景（Sn=18.8），与作者直觉相反。作者将其视为核心发现而非需解释的异常，拒绝事后调整公式，提出三种竞争性解释并预注册了判别实验设计。v2.1修订明确承认该分歧符合既有架构（偏好推断重构而非表层陈述）的预测，并新增针对信息的预注册构念效度检验。文档兼具试点报告（n=2）与下一阶段预注册协议的双重功能，不声称Sn已被验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叙事熵（Sn）此前仅有理论定义，从未在真实文本上操作化。本研究的动机是首次将该理论构念应用于真实文本，检验其可计算性与区分能力，并将"反直觉"结果本身作为核心研究问题而非测量异常来处理。

Method: 采用单一评分者手动编码法，对两个叙事场景（《落水狗》餐厅开场场景和《大教堂》开场内心独白块）进行标注，使用候选公式 Sn = I_f × C_b × t 计算评分。v2.1修订补充了针对 I_f 的预注册构念效度检验设计。

Result: 结果显示单声部独白的Sn（30.0）高于九角色对话场景（Sn=18.8），与作者直觉预期相反；同时I_f值在两场景间几乎相等（1.71 vs 1.58），但Sn出现明显差异。作者将该分歧视为核心发现，并呈现三种竞争性解释：公式不完整、真实高负荷散文、测量误差。

Conclusion: 作者拒绝将反直觉结果归因于公式缺陷或事后调整，而是将其视为需要判别实验来解析的核心发现。文档作为试点报告与预注册协议的双重载体，明确声明Sn尚未被验证，下一步将通过预注册设计在三种竞争性解释间做出判别。

Abstract: Narrative Entropy ($S_n$) is a proposed quantitative descriptor within the Bulut Doctrine, intended to capture the rate at which a narrative text imposes processing load on a reader. To date the construct has been defined theoretically but not operationalized against real texts. This report documents the first such operationalization (the v2.0 pilot): two narrative scenes -- the opening restaurant scene of Tarantino's Reservoir Dogs and the opening interior-monologue block of Carver's Cathedral -- were coded manually by a single rater and scored with the candidate formula $S_n = I_f \times C_b \times t$. The result was a divergence from the author's naive intuition: the single-voice monologue ($S_n = 30.0$) scored higher than the nine-character dialogue scene ($S_n = 18.8$). We treat this not as a result to be explained away but as the central finding, and we refuse post-hoc adjustment of the formula. Three competing interpretations are presented -- formula incompleteness, genuine high-load prose, and measurement error -- and the design that would discriminate among them is pre-registered. This v2.1 revision adds: (i) explicit acknowledgement that the divergence is consistent with the pre-existing architectural framework which privileges inferential reconstruction over surface declaration, and that what was called "contrary to expectation" in v2.0 reflected the author's anticipatory intuition rather than the methodology's own predictions; (ii) a pre-registered construct validity test for $I_f$, motivated by the observation that $I_f$ values were nearly equal across the two scenes (1.71 vs 1.58) despite the headline $S_n$ divergence. The document functions simultaneously as a pilot report ($n=2$) and as a pre-registration of the next-stage protocol. It does not claim that $S_n$ has been validated.

</details>


### [24] [Accurate Decoding of Natural Sentences from Non-Invasive Brain Recordings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18114)
*Mingfang Zhang,Jarod Lévy,Cedric Rommel,Jérémy Rapin,Corentin Bel,Julie Bonnaire,Daniel Nieto,Pierre Bourdillon,Svetlana Pinet,Stéphane d'Ascoli,Thomas Moreau,Jean-Rémi King*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Brain2Qwerty v2，一种仅依赖实时脑磁图（MEG）记录即可解码自然语句生成的模型。通过9名受试者各10小时、共22,000句的键入数据训练，模型利用字符、词和句子级表征实现平均词错误率（WER）39%，最佳参与者的半数句子解码误差不超过一个词。解码精度随数据量对数线性提升，表明非侵入式方法可通过数据扩展缩小与颅内植入方法的差距。AI在事件检测深度学习替代、大语言模型微调语义表征提取及自动化代码开发三方面驱动了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑损伤后失去语言和运动能力的患者亟需可靠的沟通恢复方案。虽然颅内植入式脑机接口已实现高性能解码，但非侵入式替代方案性能仍然滞后。论文旨在验证非侵入式脑—文本解码能否达到接近手术植入式的精度水平，从而为患者提供安全、高效的沟通途径。

Method: 收集9名受试者各10小时、共22,000句键入句子的实时MEG记录；构建Brain2Qwerty v2模型，融合字符、词、句子三级表征进行序列解码。利用深度神经网络替代传统手工事件检测管线，微调大语言模型提取语义表征，并部署AI代理通过自动化代码开发迭代优化解码流程。

Result: 平均词错误率（WER）达39%，最佳参与者中半数句子解码误差不超过一个词。解码精度随数据量对数线性提升，提示可通过数据扩展进一步缩小与颅内方法的性能差距。

Conclusion: 非侵入式脑—文本解码已开始达到此前被认为仅手术植入式可能实现的精度水平，为安全高效的脑机接口开辟了道路。数据扩展是弥合非侵入式与侵入式方法性能差距的关键路径。

Abstract: Restoring communication for people who have lost the ability to speak or move after a brain injury is a major challenge. While intracranial implants now enable high-performing brain-computer-interfaces, non-invasive alternatives are still lagging behind. Here, we present Brain2Qwerty v2, a model that can decode the production of natural sentences solely from real-time magnetoencephalography (MEG) recordings. By collecting 22,000 sentences typed by nine subjects, each recorded for 10 hours, our model leverages character, word and sentence-level representations to achieve an average word error rate (WER) of 39%. For our best participant, the model accurately decodes half of the sentences with one word error or less. Critically, decoding accuracy log-linearly improves with data volume, suggesting that the performance gap with intracranial approaches could be partially bridged through data scaling. We show that AI enables this performance in three main ways: the substitution of hand-crafted pipelines for event detection with deep learning, the finetuning of large language models to extract semantic representations, and the deployment of AI agents to iteratively refine our decoding pipeline via automated code development. Together, these results show that non-invasive brain-to-text decoding starts to operate at a level of accuracy previously thought exclusive to surgical implants, opening a path toward safe and efficient brain-computer-interfaces.

</details>


### [25] [Are LLMs Safe Beyond Text: Do Emojis Expose Gaps in Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18164)
*M P V S Gopinadh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型在表情符号增强提示下的安全鲁棒性，发现不同模型对非标准输入表示的防御能力存在显著差异：Qwen 2 7B完全抵抗（0%成功率），Gemma 2 9B和Mistral 7B成功率10%，Llama 3 8B为6%，卡方检验证实差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全评估主要依赖基于文本的对抗性提示，可能忽略由替代输入表示（如表情符号）引发的安全漏洞，需要检验这一评估空白。

Method: 使用50个提示作为测试样本，对四个开源LLM（Mistral 7B、Qwen 2 7B、Gemma 2 9B、Llama 3 8B）进行表情符号增强提示的安全评估，并采用卡方检验验证结果分布差异的统计显著性。

Result: Gemma 2 9B和Mistral 7B的成功率为10%，Llama 3 8B为6%，Qwen 2 7B为0%；卡方检验显示χ²=32.94，p<0.001，模型间结果分布差异显著。

Conclusion: 模型鲁棒性对输入表示形式敏感，仅依赖标准文本提示的安全评估可能低估模型的实际漏洞，安全评测需要涵盖更多元化的输入表示。

Abstract: Safety evaluations of large language models (LLMs) predominantly rely on text-based adversarial prompts, potentially overlooking vulnerabilities arising from alternative input representations. This work examines emoji-augmented prompts as a test case for this gap, evaluating 50 prompts across four open-source LLMs (Mistral 7B, Qwen 2 7B, Gemma 2 9B, Llama 3 8B). Results show substantial variation in robustness: Gemma 2 9B and Mistral 7B exhibit non-zero success rates (10%), Llama 3 8B 6%, while Qwen 2 7B shows complete resistance (0% success rate). A chi-square test ($χ^2 = 32.94, p < 0.001$) confirms significant differences in outcome distributions. These findings indicate that robustness is sensitive to input representation, and that evaluations restricted to standard text prompts may underrepresent model vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [26] [Temporal Multi-Signal Fusion for Token-Level Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18115)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将幻觉检测视为时间上扩展的片段的序列标注问题，而非逐token独立评分。该方法基于33维特征流（融合文本统计、NLI蕴含和语言模型surprisal），使用BiGRU在RAGTruth上达到0.840的AUC，比独立逻辑回归基线提升11个点，且不依赖模型内部信息，可推广到闭源模型和未见过的语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐token级别的幻觉检测器对每个token独立打分，仅依赖单一信号，在生成模型自信但错误时（confidently wrong）会失效。现有方法缺乏对时间跨度的建模，无法利用span内上下文证据传播。

Method: 将幻觉检测构建为序列标注任务：每个token从33维特征流评分，特征融合文本统计、NLI蕴含关系和语言模型surprisal，无需访问模型内部。使用双向门控循环单元（BiGRU）对特征流建模，并通过受控消融实验分解时间顺序与模型容量的贡献。

Result: 在RAGTruth数据集上（10个种子）达到0.840的AUC，比独立逻辑回归基线提升11个百分点（p=0.002，Wilcoxon符号秩检验）。消融表明主要增益来自时间顺序而非模型容量，证据从自信位置传播到模糊邻居。在循环、状态空间（Mamba）和注意力架构上均达到约0.845的天花板，瓶颈在特征集而非模型。对训练中未见过的语言模型生成的文本，AUC损失低于4%。

Conclusion: 幻觉检测的瓶颈在于特征集的设计而非模型架构；时间维度上的证据传播是提升检测效果的关键因素。由于仅依赖生成文本和外部信号，该方法适用于闭源模型及跨模型泛化场景。

Abstract: Token-level hallucination detectors score each token independently from a single signal, and fail exactly when the generating model is confidently wrong. This paper instead treats hallucination as a temporally extended span and detects it by sequence labeling: each token is scored from a 33-dimensional feature stream that fuses text statistics, Natural Language Inference (NLI) entailment, and language model surprisal, with no access to model internals. A Bidirectional Gated Recurrent Unit (BiGRU) over these features reaches an AUC of 0.840 on RAGTruth (10 seeds), an 11-point gain over an independent logistic-regression baseline (p = 0.002, Wilcoxon signed-rank). A controlled decomposition attributes most of the gain to temporal order rather than model capacity: evidence propagates from confident positions to ambiguous neighbors within a span. The same 0.845 ceiling recurs across recurrent, state-space (Mamba), and attention architectures, locating the bottleneck in the feature set rather than the model. Because it reads only the generated text and external signals, the detector works on closed-source models, and it keeps working on text produced by language models it never saw during training, losing under 4% AUC.

</details>


### [27] [You Are What You Prompt: Prompt Quality, Domain Shift, and Uncertainty in Agrifood Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18116)
*Andrea Morales-Garzón,Salvador López-Joya,Miguel López-Pérez,Maria J. Martin-Bautista*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了零样本提示集成（ZPE）在农业食品领域的域转移场景下的表现，发现ZPE在域外条件下显著提升性能和校准，并提出PID方法利用提示不一致性作为认知不确定性来改善故障检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型虽能通过自然语言提示实现零样本分类，但提示公式化对性能影响显著，尤其在专业领域。ZPE虽通过判别性信号加权提示解决此问题，但其在域转移下的行为尚未被探索。

Method: 使用CLIP和SigLIP在四个数据集和四个提示池上评估ZPE，覆盖分布内（ID）食品和分布外（OOD）农业基准。同时提出PID（Prompt-based Inconsistency Detection）方法，将提示之间的不一致性重新定义为认知不确定性，用于改进故障检测。

Result: ZPE在ID条件下益处有限，但在域转移下大幅提升性能和校准；领域特定提示池（51-52个）优于通用提示池（247-426个）。词法分析表明ZPE可作为无监督的域对齐检测器。在严重域转移下，标准置信度失效时，PID改善了故障检测。

Conclusion: ZPE在域转移场景中是一个有效的提示加权策略，能够充当无监督域对齐检测器；PID通过提示不一致性提供的认知不确定性，在标准置信度失效的情况下显著提升故障检测能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models enable zero-shot classification through natural language prompts, but performance is sensitive to prompt formulation, especially in specialized domains. Zero-shot Prompt Ensembling (ZPE) addresses this by weighting prompts by discriminative signal, yet its behavior under domain shift remains unexplored. We evaluate ZPE in the agrifood domain using CLIP and SigLIP across four datasets and four prompt pools, spanning in-distribution (ID) food and out-of-distribution agricultural benchmarks. ZPE provides limited benefit under ID conditions but substantially improves performance and calibration under domain shift, where domain-specific pools of 51-52 prompts consistently outperform generic pools of 247-426. Lexical analysis shows that ZPE acts as an unsupervised domain-alignment detector without label access. We further introduce PID (Prompt-based Inconsistency Detection), which repurposes prompt disagreement as epistemic uncertainty, improving failure detection under severe domain shift where standard confidence measures collapse.

</details>


### [28] [Alignment Is All You Need: Instruction-Free Training for General Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18132)
*Xuanru Zhou,Yiwen Shao,Jiahong Li,Dong Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需指令、仅需对齐的轻量级大型音频-语言模型（LALM），保持音频编码器和LLM完全冻结，仅训练一个轻量级投影器，通过自生成数据构造的(audio, response)对进行训练，在多模态基准上匹敌或超越重度后训练的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MLLM构建依赖多阶段流程（跨模态对齐、SFT、偏好优化），需要大量任务特定监督。但预训练LLM已具备强大的推理和指令跟随能力，作者质疑是否可以通过最小干预高效地将这些能力迁移到新模态，仅靠对齐是否足以构建多模态模型。

Method: 提出Instruction-Free Alignment-Only的LALM：音频编码器和LLM完全冻结，仅学习轻量投影器。借鉴AzeroS方法，使用自生成数据构造（Self-Generated Data Construction），让LLM将字幕扩展为自由形式响应，不依赖显式任务指令，训练(audio, response)对。

Result: 在MMAU、MMAR、MMSU、MMAU-Pro四个基准上，该方法以显著较少的数据匹敌或超越广泛后训练的基线模型。保持LLM冻结保留了其原生指令跟随能力，并可在不同模型代际间无缝迁移。

Conclusion: 竞争性的MLLM可以仅通过对齐训练涌现，将多模态扩展简化为一个轻量级投影器训练问题，该方案跨模态泛化良好，并能快速适配每个新的LLM发布版本。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are typically built through a multi-stage pipeline consisting of cross-modal alignment, supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and preference optimization. This pipeline assumes that adapting an LLM to a new modality requires extensive task-specific supervision. However, pretrained LLMs already possess strong reasoning and instruction-following abilities. As LLMs evolve rapidly, an important question remains: can we efficiently transfer these capabilities to a new modality with minimal intervention, and is alignment alone sufficient for building a multimodal model? We introduce an Instruction-Free Alignment-Only large audio-language model (LALM) that keeps both the audio encoder and the LLM fully frozen, learning only a lightweight projector. Borrowing insights from AzeroS [1], we train on (audio, response) pairs from Self-Generated Data Construction, where an LLM expands captions into free-form responses without explicit task instructions. Across MMAU, MMAR, MMSU, and MMAU-Pro, our approach matches or surpasses heavily post-trained baselines using substantially less data. By keeping the LLM frozen, our model preserves its native instruction-following competence and can port seamlessly across model generations. Our results suggest that competitive MLLM can emerge from alignment alone, reducing multimodal extension to a lightweight projector-training problem that generalizes across modalities and adapts rapidly to each new LLM release.

</details>
